Let's define functions:
def corr_sums(h): n=len(h); L=n-k.
  A = [sum h[:L]*(1-h[k:]) for k in 0..n-1] where A[0]=sum h*(1-h) = n*mean - sum h^2.
  B = [sum h[k:]* (1-h[:L])].
Then all c = [A[k], B[k]]. For k=0 A=B=c0 duplicate.
We can compute A/B efficiently O(n^2) or FFT.
A_k = sum_{i=0}^{L-1} h_i - sum h_i h_{i+k}. B_k = sum_{i=k}^{n-1} h_i - sum h_i h_{i+k}. Let prefix sums H. Sum_h_A = H[n-k] (prefix up to n-k? exclusive). Sum_h_B = H[n] - H[k]. Q_k = ∑ h_i h_{i+k} (autocorr of h). Then A_k = prefix(n-k) - Q_k, B_k = total - prefix(k) - Q_k. Much simpler! No bilinear gradient? Q_k gradient involves products. For final c easy.

For continuous v: A_k = ( (n-k + V1 - V2) - S_v)/4 etc. But h form easier.

A_k, B_k are linear in h minus quadratic Q_k. Gradient:
∂A_k/∂h_i = δ[i < n-k] - ∂Q_k/∂h_i.
∂Q_k/∂h_i = h_{i-k} if i>=k + h_{i+k} if i<n-k. (since Q_k=sum h_i h_{i+k}).
Similarly ∂B_k/∂h_i = δ[i >= k] - same ∂Q_k/∂h_i.
Then weighted gradient:
G_i = ∑_{k} [wA_k δ(i<n-k) + wB_k δ(i>=k)] - ∑_{k} (wA_k+wB_k) (h_{i-k}+h_{i+k}). We can compute if weights sparse or via convolution.

FISTA anneal implementation details:
```
def fista_anneal(n, h0, rng, max_time, betas=None):
  h = make_feasible(h0,n)
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h)
  if betas is None: scales=[1,5,20,100,500,2000]
  start_t=time.time()
  for scale in scales:
     if time_left<60: break
     beta=scale/n
     h, c = fista_lse(n,h,beta,iters= some based on n,time)
     best update
  return best_h,best_c
```
FISTA for a given beta:
  step0 = max(0.1, 2.0/(beta*dx+0.01))? For n=160 dx=.0125, beta final 12.5 => denom .156 =>12.8; cap 5.0. Low beta=1/160=.006 => denom .000? step large maybe 5. Use step0=min(5.0, 2.0/(max(dx*(beta+1),1e-3))). For high beta 5? Good.
  t=1; y=h.copy(); x=h.copy(); L_y,grad_y = lse_and_grad(y,beta)
  step=step0
  for it in range(iters):
     if time > limit: break
     # smoothed line search
     for _ in range(10):
        z = project_box_sum(y - step*grad_y, target)
        Lz = lse_eval(z,beta)
        if Lz <= L_y + 1e-13: accept; break
        step *=0.5
     if no accept: z=y; step*=0.1? break?
     t_new = 0.5*(1+sqrt(1+3*t*t))
     y_try = z + ((t-1)/t_new)*(z - x)
     # For nonconvex, if y_try not feasible/project? FISTA y not necessarily feasible; projection on step. Keep y maybe can be outside [0,1]; that's okay, but projection at next.
     x = z; y = y_try; t=t_new
     every 20 compute exact C of x, update best.
     # adapt step: if accepted first, maybe increase by 1.1? Or maintain.
  return
```

Let's implement SLSQP anneal carefully.
```
def slsqp_polish(n, h0, max_time, betas_scales):
  best_h=make_feasible(h0,n); best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
  for scale in [20,100,500,2000,10000?]:
    if time_left<30 break
    beta=scale/n
    _GLOBAL['beta']=beta
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'jac':sum_jac}]
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    try:
       res = minimize(slsqp_fun, best_h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, jac=slsqp_grad, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':200,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
       best_h=make_feasible(res.x,n); c=compute; update
    except: pass
  return best_h,best_c
```

Objective exact C computed by `compute_c5` with np.correlate. Let's make it fast and exact:
```
def compute_c5(h):
  n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
  corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
  return float(np.max(corr))*dx
```

Pseudo:
```
_OPT = {'beta':1.0, 'mu':1.0, 'target':0.0, 'n':0}

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start=time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # parent
    h_best = None
    try:
       hp = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
       if hp.size>0:
          h_best = np.array(hp, copy=True)
    except Exception: pass
    if h_best is None: h_best = initial_construction_fallback(...)
    n_best = len(h_best)
    h_best = make_feasible(h_best, n_best)
    c_best = compute_c5(h_best)
    # clean initial candidates if time
    if budget_s - (time.time()-start) > 300:
       # generate candidates at n_best and maybe 2* n_best, select top starts
    # Phase 1 optimize n_best
    n_cur=n_best
    h_cur=make_feasible(h_best,n_cur)
    # FISTA anneal (time based)
    h_cur,c_cur = fista_anneal(n_cur,h_cur,rng, time_limit = min(300, ...))
    if c_cur < c_best: update
    # SLSQP polish
    if n_cur <= 300 and time_left>120:
       h_s,c_s = slsqp_anneal(n_cur, make_feasible(h_cur,n_cur), max_time=...)
       update
    h_cur=make_feasible(...)
    # local search
    if time_left>250:
       h_l,c_l = local_sa(n_cur,h_cur,rng, time_budget=...)
       update
    # Phase 2 other resolutions
    for n_try in [2*n_cur if <=512?, 240,320,512] unique sorted:
       if time_left<120: break
       if abs(n_try-n_cur)<1: continue
       h_init = resample(h_best,n_try) # use current best
       # maybe candidate different at n_try
       h_try,c_try = fista_anneal(n_try,h_init,rng,time_limit=max(30, min(240, time_left-60)))
       if improved update
       if n_try <= 300 and time_left>120: SLSQP polish
       if time_left>120: local small
    # final repair and re-evaluate
    h_best = make_feasible(h_best,n_best)
    c_best = compute_c5(h_best)
    # maybe try downsample if n_best>160? Return best n.
    assert ...
    return (h_best,c_best,n_best)
```
Need no nested functions; loops okay.

- If no improvements, still local.

   If time_left > 400: fista limit 250; slsqp 120; local 200.

In grad:
  W = wA+wB
  # If W all zero (should not), quadratic none.
  fa = np.fft.rfft(W[1:], N)

  left = conv[:n]
  fa_rev = np.fft.rfft(W[1:][::-1], N)
  convr = np.fft.irfft(fa_rev * f, N).real
  right = convr[n-1:n-1+n]
  # Note f is rfft(h,N). Good.
  # term:
  idx = np.arange(n)
  cumA = np.cumsum(wA)
  cumB = np.cumsum(wB)
  term = p0 + cumA[n-1-idx] + cumB[idx]
  G = term - (2.0*p0*h + left + right)
  grad = dx * G
  # If numerical, replace nan.
  if not finite: fallback direct? Could set grad=0.
  return L, grad
```

    if time elapsed >max_time or time_left? no global budget inside? We'll pass max_time and track start.
    if n large maybe scales fewer.
    beta=scale/n
    _OPT['beta']=beta
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'jac':sum_jac}]

        h=h2
    except Exception:
      pass
  return best_h,best_c

def fista_lse(n,h0,beta,max_time,base_step=5.0):
  h=make_feasible(h0,n); best=c; x=h.copy(); x_prev=x.copy()
  t=1.0; y=h.copy(); step=base_step; base=base_step
  L_y, grad_y = lse_and_grad(y,beta)
  for it in range(50000):
    elapsed=...
    if elapsed>max_time: break
    # optional stop if grad norm tiny
    accepted=False
    current_step=step
    for _ in range(8):
       z = project_box_sum(y - current_step*grad_y, n/2)
       Lz = lse_eval(z,beta)
       if Lz <= L_y + 1e-13:
           accepted=True; break
       current_step *=0.5
    if not accepted:
       base = max(1e-3, base*0.5)
       step=base
       # if base tiny break
       if base < 1e-6: break
       continue
    step = min(10.0, step*1.2) # maybe increase
    t_new = 0.5*(1+sqrt(1+3t*t))
    y_try = z + ((t-1)/t_new)*(z - x)
    y = project_box_sum(y_try, n/2)
    x=z; t=t_new
    if it%20==0:
       c=compute_c5(x); if c<best ...
       L_y, grad_y = lse_and_grad(y,beta)
    else:
       # update L_y every iter? Need L_y for next line search. We can compute after y projection.
       L_y, grad_y = lse_and_grad(y,beta)
  # final exact
  c=compute_c5(x); update
  return best_h,best_c
```

  x=make_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c5(x); best=x.copy(); best_c=c
  target=n/2

  total_iters = estimate: n*2000? if n=160 320k; but time limited.
  for it in range(max iterations until time):
    if time>max_time: break
    T = t_start * (t_end/t_start)**(it/total_iters)
    step = max(0.001, 0.1 * (0.5)**(it/total_iters))? Step anneal
    i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i==j continue

`make_feasible` implement robust and fast. Need ensure exact sum. Approach:
```
def make_feasible(h,n):
  h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
  h = np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2.0
  for _ in range(10):
     s=float(np.sum(h)); diff=target-s
     if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
     if diff > 0:
        mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt ==0: break
        add = diff/cnt
        # if add <=1-1e-12? Use min with 1
        h[mask] = np.minimum(1.0, h[mask] + add)
     else:
        mask = h > 1e-12
        cnt=...
        if cnt==0: break
        h[mask]=np.maximum(0.0, h[mask] + diff/cnt)
  # If still off due to clipping, bisection
  if abs(np.sum(h)-target) > 1e-9:
     lo = -2.0; hi=2.0? Need choose such that sum clip(h-tau) crosses target.
     # We want s(tau)=sum(clip(h-tau,0,1)) decreasing.
     # max tau gives 0 if tau>=max(h)+?; min tau gives n if tau<=min(h)-1?
     lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0 - target  # ensures x - lo >=1? If min h - lo >=1+target >0, clip maybe some <1? Actually to get sum n, need tau <= min(h)-1. If tau = min(h)-1 - target, then min(h)-tau =1+target, clip all 1 -> sum n. Good.
     hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0 + target # max - tau = -1-target -> all 0.
     for _ in range(60):
        mid=(lo+hi)/2
        s=float(np.sum(np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0)))
        if s > target: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
     tau=(lo+hi)/2
     h=np.clip(h-tau,0.0,1.0)
  # final tiny correction on one coordinate to force sum exactly up to floating
  s=float(np.sum(h)); diff=target-s
  if abs(diff)>0:
      # find nonboundary
      for _ in range(3):
         if diff>0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)) # but argmax of bool returns first True? yes if any, else n? Actually np.argmax(bool) returns first True or 0.
            ok = h[idx] < 1.0 - 1e-12
         else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h > 1e-12))? argmin returns first min; need condition.
      # simpler loop over list? n up to 512, okay.
      for i in range(n):
         if diff>0 and h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-12:
             val = min(1.0, h[i]+diff)
             h[i]=val; break
         elif diff<0 and h[i] > 1e-12:
             val = max(0.0, h[i]+diff)
             h[i]=val; break
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  return h
```
Final correction may not make sum exactly due to float but close. If diff large and one coord not enough, loop few; but prior bisection should. We can instead adjust uniform on free again. Fine.

One subtlety: Bisection `lo` should be lower tau giving high sum (all ones). Since s decreasing in tau. If s > target, need increase tau? Wait if tau low, sum high. In bisection, if s>target, root is higher tau, so lo=mid? Let's define lo low tau (sum high), hi high tau (sum low). If s at mid > target, mid too low -> lo=mid. Else hi=mid. Good.

def resample(h_src,n_dst):
    if len(h_src)==n_dst: return h_src.copy()
    if len(h_src)<2: return np.ones(n_dst)*0.5
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(h_src),endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_dst,endpoint=False)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h_src)
    return y
```

def generate_candidates(n,rng,max_count=10):

def local_anneal(n,h0,max_time,seed=0):
   rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
   x=make_feasible(h0,n)
   c_cur=compute_c5(x); best=x.copy(); best_c=c_cur
   target=n/2.0
   start=time.time(); elapsed0=0
   step=0.08
   T=0.05
   no_accept=0
   step_min=0.0005
   T_min=0.0002
   max_iter = 1000000
   for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()-start > max_time: break
       if it % 1000 == 0:
           frac = min(1.0, it/50000.0)
           step = max(step_min, 0.08 * (0.98 ** (it/1000.0)))? Use formula.
           T = max(T_min, 0.05 * (0.98 ** (it/1000.0)))
       if no_accept > 100:
           step=max(step_min, step*0.8); T=max(T_min,T*0.8); no_accept=0
       # choose move
       if rng.random() < 0.25:
           # multi perturb
           y=x.copy()
           m=int(rng.randint(1,5))
           idxs=rng.choice(n,m,replace=False)
           for idx in idxs:
               y[idx] = y[idx] + rng.uniform(-step, step)
           y=make_feasible(y,n)
       else:
           i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
           if i==j: continue
           amt=rng.uniform(0.0, max(step,1e-6))
           if rng.random() <0.5:
               src=i; dst=j
           else:
               src=j; dst=i
           max_move = min(amt, float(x[src]), 1.0 - float(x[dst]))
           if max_move <= 1e-8: continue
           y=x.copy()
           y[src] -= max_move
           y[dst] += max_move
       c_new=compute_c5(y)
       if c_new < c_cur or (c_cur < c_new and math.exp(-(c_new-c_cur)/max(T,1e-9)) < rng.random()):
           x=y; c_cur=c_new; no_accept=0
           if c_new < best_c:
               best_c=c_new; best=x.copy()
       else:
           no_accept +=1
       if it % 2000 ==0 and c_cur > best_c + 0.004:
           x=best.copy(); c_cur=best_c
   # final if current better? best already
   return best, best_c
```
Accept condition reversed: Should accept if rng.random() < exp(-...), not exp < rng. Use `rng.random() < math.exp(...)`. Need ensure exp arg not underflow; if c_new-c_cur large, exp=0.
T values: C differences scale 1e-4; T start 0.05 accepts many worse; maybe too high, but SA can explore. For continuous pair moves, C changes maybe 1e-4. T=0.05 always accepts; may wander. Could use T start 0.005. Use Tstart=0.005, Tend=1e-4. Step start 0.05. Good.

Now fista gradient plain.
```
def fista_lse(n,h0,beta,max_time,base_step=1.0):
   start=time.time()
   x=make_feasible(h0,n)
   best=x.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(x)
   step=base_step
   base=base_step
   stuck=0
   for it in range(100000):
      if time.time()-start > max_time: break
      L, grad = lse_and_grad(x,beta)
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
      # optional grad norm threshold
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(10):
         y = project_box_sum(x - step*grad, n)
         Ly = lse_eval(y,beta)
         if Ly <= L + 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(Ly):
             accepted=True; break
         step *=0.5
      if not accepted:
         base = max(1e-4, base*0.5)
         step=base
         stuck +=1
         if stuck > 20: break
         continue
      stuck=0
      step = min(5.0, step*1.05)
      x=y
      if it % 20 ==0 or it==0:
         c=compute_c5(x)
         if c < best_c:
             best_c=c; best=x.copy()
   c=compute_c5(x); if c<best: update
   return best,best_c
```
If `Ly` is nan, maybe accept? no, break. Use finite.

```
def project_box_sum(x,n):
  h=np.asarray(x,dtype=float); h=np.clip(h,0,1); target=n/2.0
  for _ in range(6):
    s=float(np.sum(h)); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
    if diff>0:
       mask=h<1.0-1e-12; cnt=...
       if cnt==0: break
       h[mask]=np.minimum(1.0,h[mask]+diff/cnt)
    else: ...
  if abs(float(np.sum(h))-target)>1e-8:
     # fallback full make_feasible
     h=make_feasible(h,n)
  return h
```

def fista_anneal(n,h0,max_time,seed=42):
  start=time.time()
  x=make_feasible(h0,n); best=x.copy(); best_c=compute
  scales=(2,10,50,200,1000,3000)

Detailed `run`:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h_best = None
    try:
        hp = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if hp.size > 0:
            h_best = hp.copy().reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        h_best = None
    if h_best is None:
        # fallback structured h: use Wiedemann 160
        n_fallback=160
        h_best=np.zeros(n_fallback)
        for i in range(n_fallback):
           t=(i*(i+1)//2)%n_fallback
           if t < n_fallback//2: h_best[i]=1.0
    n_best = len(h_best)
    if n_best < 2: h_best = np.ones(160)*0.5; n_best=160
    h_best = make_feasible(h_best, n_best)
    c_best = compute_c5(h_best)
    rem = budget_s - (time.time()-start_time)
    # Alternative starts at n_best if time
    alt_h = None
    if rem > 350 and n_best <= 256:
       cands = generate_candidates(n_best, rng, 12)
       best_alt_c = c_best + 0.02
       for h_cand in cands:
           if time.time()... skip? compute
           c_cand=compute_c5(h_cand)
           if c_cand < best_alt_c and c_cand < (c_best if alt_h is None? no):
              # Keep best alternative distinct? just min
              if alt_h is None or c_cand < best_alt_c:
                  best_alt_c=c_cand; alt_h=h_cand
       # If no alt within margin but c_cand < c_best? Maybe parent already; we need alternative different. Keep min c if < c_best+0.05 even if >c_best.
    # Main n_best FISTA
    if rem > 150:
       fista_time = min(240.0, max(30.0, rem - 200.0))
       h_f, final_f, c_f = fista_anneal(n_best, h_best, fista_time)
       if c_f < c_best: h_best=h_f; c_best=c_f
       elif final_f is not None:
           # maybe use final for slsqp? keep best
       rem = budget_s - (time.time()-start_time)
    if rem > 120 and n_best <= 260:
       slsqp_time = min(120.0, max(20.0, rem - 80.0))
       h_s, c_s = slsqp_anneal(n_best, h_best, slsqp_time)
       if c_s < c_best: h_best=h_s; c_best=c_s
       rem=...
    if rem > 200:
       local_time = min(180.0, max(30.0, rem - 100.0))
       h_l, c_l = local_anneal(n_best, h_best, local_time, seed=seed+1)
       if c_l < c_best: h_best=h_l; c_best=c_l
       rem=...
    # alternative start optimize if time
    rem=...
    if rem > 120 and alt_h is not None and len(alt_h)==n_best and c_best > 0.38085? even if not, if time:
       alt_time = min(120.0, max(30.0, rem - 60.0))
       h_a, final_a, c_a = fista_anneal(n_best, alt_h, alt_time)
       if c_a < c_best: update
       rem=...
       if rem>80 and n_best<=260:
          h_as,c_as = slsqp_anneal(n_best,h_best,min(60,rem-30))
          if c_as < c_best update
    # other resolutions
    n_opts = []
    base=n_best
    for v in [int(base*1.5), base*2, 240, 320, 512, 640]:
       if v < 64: continue
       if v > 1024: continue
       if v % 2 !=0: v+=1
       if len(n_opts)<... and v not in n_opts and v != base: n_opts.append(v)
    # sort without lambda: n_opts.sort()
    for n_try in n_opts:
       rem = budget_s - (time.time()-start_time)
       if rem < 120: break
       h_init = resample(h_best, n_try)
       fista_time = min(120.0, max(20.0, rem - 80.0))
       h_f, final_f, c_f = fista_anneal(n_try, h_init, fista_time)
       if c_f < c_best:
          h_best = h_f; c_best=c_f; n_best=n_try
       rem=...
       if rem > 100 and n_try <= 260:
           slsqp_time=min(70.0, max(15.0, rem-50.0))
           h_s,c_s=slsqp_anneal(n_try,h_best,slsqp_time)
           if c_s<c_best: update
       rem=...
       if rem > 80 and c_best > 0? and n_try <= 400:
           local_time=min(60.0, max(10.0, rem-30.0))
           h_l,c_l=local_anneal(n_try,h_best,local_time,seed=seed+n_try)
           if c_l<c_best: update
    # final polish best n with FISTA high beta + local if time
    rem=...
    if rem > 80:
       h_f, final_f, c_f = fista_anneal(n_best,h_best,min(60.0,rem-20.0)) # but fista_anneal runs all scales; maybe too much. Could call fista_lse high beta direct. We don't have a single phase function. We can call slsqp or local.

    h_best=make_feasible(h_best,n_best)
    c_best=compute_c5(h_best)
    return (h_best, float(c_best), int(n_best))

Now, let's review mathematical correctness of gradient with LSE weights and dx. Objective `L = dx * (m + (log s - log num)/β)`. Derivative: dx/β * (s'/s) = dx * ∑ (s_l/s) * c_l' since s_l=e^{β(c_l-m)}, s'_l=β e^{...} c_l'. Yes weights. Good.

Gradient formula for c_l as A/B: Need ensure derivative of c_l = A_k = prefix - Q. Our formula uses term1 from linear prefix and subtract Q derivative. We set A_k sum over h_i i=0..n-k-1; derivative +1 for i<n-k. B_k sum over h_i i=k..n-1; derivative +1 for i>=k. Q_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-k-1} h_i h_{i+k}; derivative w.r.t h_j: if j+k<n, h_{j+k} (as first factor) + if j>=k, h_{j-k} (as second factor from i=j-k first? Q sum h_i h_{i+k}, if j=i+k => i=j-k, derivative second factor h_i=h_{j-k}; if j=i => h_{j+k}). Good. So G = ∑wA deltaA + ∑wB deltaB - ∑(wA+wW)Qderiv - c0 derivative Q0? c0=∑h_i - h_i^2; derivative 1-2h_i. We use p0*(1-2h). Good. Weighted by dx.

Line search uses gradient of L with respect to h without projection; projection after step. For nonconvex, okay.

Could there be issue with `left` and `right` from FFT: We compute convolution `a * h` with a length n-1. For left_i = ∑_{m=0}^i a[m] h[i-m], where a[m]=W[m+1]. Good. For i> n? Take first n. For right_i = ∑_{k=1}^{n-1-i} W[k] h[i+k]. Convolution `a_rev * h`, a_rev[m]=W[n-1-m] for m=0..n-2. Slice index n-1+i. Let's verify n=5, i=2: want W1 h3 + W2 h4. a_rev: [W4,W3,W2,W1]. conv index 6? n-1+i=6.
conv[6]=∑_m a_rev[m] h[6-m]. m=0 h6 invalid 0; m=1 aW3 h5 invalid; m=2 W2 h4; m=3 W1 h3. correct. For i=0: index4. m=0 W4 h4, m1 W3 h3,m2 W2 h2,m3 W1 h1. correct. Good.

Now, if W has zeros, still compute convolution. Could skip if `W_sum < 1e-15` (only p0 weights). Then left/right zero. But p0 plus maybe? If high beta and max c0, weights only p0; W zero. In grad, G=p0*(1-2h). Good. Need still compute left/right? skip if sumW==0 to save and avoid. Implement:

W = wA + wB

   compute

```

Let's code `fista_anneal` returning current final. Implementation:
```
def fista_anneal(n,h0,max_time):
    start=time.time()
    x=make_feasible(h0,n)
    best_h=x.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(x)
    scales=(2,10,50,200,1000,3000)
    weights=(1,2,3,4,5,4)
    total_w=float(sum(weights))
    for idx in range(len(scales)):
        elapsed=time.time()-start
        if max_time - elapsed < 5.0: break
        w=weights[idx]
        slice=max(2.0, max_time*w/total_w)
        # cap by remaining
        slice=min(slice, max_time - elapsed)
        if slice < 3.0: continue? break
        beta=float(scales[idx])/float(n)
        best_phase, final_phase, c_phase = fista_lse(n,x,beta,slice)
        if c_phase < best_c:
            best_c=c_phase; best_h=best_phase.copy()
        # choose next start
        if c_phase + 0.01 >= best_c:
            x=final_phase
        else:
            x=best_phase
    return best_h, x, best_c
```
If `slice` negative? break.

def resample(h_src,n_dst):
   if len(h_src)==n_dst: return copy
   if n_dst % len(h_src) == 0:

       return np.repeat(h_src, m)

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)) * dx
```

`next_power_two`.

`lse_and_grad`:
```
L,p0,pA,pB,f,N = lse_internal(h,beta,True)
n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
wA=np.zeros(n); wB=np.zeros(n)
if n>1: wA[1:]=pA; wB[1:]=pB
W=wA+wB
term = np.empty(n)
idx = np.arange(n)
cumA = np.cumsum(wA); cumB = np.cumsum(wB)
term = p0 + cumA[n-1-idx] + cumB[idx]
Wsum = float(np.sum(W))
quad = 2.0*p0*h
if Wsum > 1e-15:
   a = W[1:]
   fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
   conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * f, N).real
   left = conv[:n]
   ar = a[::-1]
   fa_rev = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
   convr = np.fft.irfft(fa_rev * f, N).real
   right = convr[n-1:n-1+n]
   quad = quad + left + right
G = term - quad
grad = dx * G
# sanitize
if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
   grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
return L, grad
```
Note in lse_internal, h is clipped and possibly new array; `f` corresponds to clipped h. In grad, we need use same h array. lse_internal should return clipped h too? It doesn't currently. We can return h? Or in lse_and_grad clip h ourselves consistent. Better: lse_and_grad:
```
h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
L,p0,pA,pB,f,N = lse_internal(h,beta,True)
```
Then use h. lse_internal should not reclip? It will, okay. But if it returns f from its clipped h; same.

```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_OPT = {'beta': 1.0}


def remaining_time(start_time, budget_s):
    return float(budget_s) - (time.time() - float(start_time))


def make_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if len(h) != n:
        # Should not happen; fall back to safe resampling.
        if len(h) == 1:
            h = np.full(n, float(h[0]))
        else:
            if len(h) > n:
                h = h[:n]
            else:
                pad = np.zeros(n - len(h), dtype=float)
                h = np.concatenate((h, pad))
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0

    for _ in range(12):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] = np.minimum(1.0, h[mask] + diff / cnt)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] = np.maximum(0.0, h[mask] + diff / cnt)

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(np.sum(h)) - target) > 1e-9:
        lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0 - target
        hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0 + target
        for _ in range(70):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = float(np.sum(np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
            if s > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        h = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    s = float(np.sum(h))
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        for i in range(n):
            if diff > 0.0 and h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-12:
                h[i] = min(1.0, h[i] + diff)
                break
            if diff < 0.0 and h[i] > 1e-12:
                h[i] = max(0.0, h[i] + diff)
                break
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    n = int(h.size)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)) * dx


def next_power_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def lse_internal(x, beta, need_weights):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size == 0:
        return 0.0, None, None, None, None, None
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    n = int(h.size)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    N = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)

    f = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(f * np.conj(f), N).real
    Q = ac[:n].copy()
    if n > 0:
        Q[0] = float(np.dot(h, h))
    Q = np.maximum(Q, 0.0)

    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    if n > 0:
        np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])

    total = float(pref[n])
    c0 = total - Q[0]
    c0 = max(0.0, float(c0))

    A = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    B = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    A[0] = c0
    B[0] = c0
    max_c = c0
    for k in range(1, n):
        valA = pref[n - k] - Q[k]
        valB = total - pref[k] - Q[k]
        valA = max(0.0, float(valA))
        valB = max(0.0, float(valB))
        A[k] = valA
        B[k] = valB
        if valA > max_c:
            max_c = valA
        if valB > max_c:
            max_c = valB

    num = 2 * n - 1
    beta = float(beta)

    if beta < 1e-7:
        if n == 1:
            mean_c = c0
        else:
            mean_c = (c0 + float(np.sum(A[1:])) + float(np.sum(B[1:]))) / float(num)
        L = dx * mean_c
        if need_weights:
            inv = 1.0 / float(num)
            p0 = inv
            pA = np.full(max(n - 1, 0), inv, dtype=float)
            pB = np.full(max(n - 1, 0), inv, dtype=float)
            return L, p0, pA, pB, f, N
        return L, None, None, None, None, N

    z0 = beta * (c0 - max_c)
    exp0 = float(np.exp(z0))

    if n > 1:
        zA = beta * (A[1:] - max_c)
        zB = beta * (B[1:] - max_c)
        expA = np.exp(zA)
        expB = np.exp(zB)
        s = exp0 + float(np.sum(expA)) + float(np.sum(expB))
    else:
        expA = None
        expB = None
        s = exp0

    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        s = 1.0
        exp0 = 1.0
        if n > 1:
            expA = np.zeros(max(n - 1, 0), dtype=float)
            expB = np.zeros(max(n - 1, 0), dtype=float)

    inv_s = 1.0 / float(s)
    p0 = exp0 * inv_s
    L = dx * (max_c + (math.log(float(s)) - math.log(float(num))) / beta)

    if need_weights:
        pA = np.full(max(n - 1, 0), 0.0, dtype=float)
        pB = np.full(max(n - 1, 0), 0.0, dtype=float)
        if n > 1:
            pA[:] = expA * inv_s
            pB[:] = expB * inv_s
        return L, p0, pA, pB, f, N
    return L, None, None, None, None, N


def lse_eval(x, beta):
    L, _, _, _, _, _ = lse_internal(x, beta, False)
    return float(L)


def lse_and_grad(x, beta):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / n

    L, p0, pA, pB, f, N = lse_internal(h, beta, True)

    wA = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    wB = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        wA[1:] = pA
        wB[1:] = pB

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    cumA = np.cumsum(wA)
    cumB = np.cumsum(wB)
    term = float(p0) + cumA[n - 1 - idx] + cumB[idx]

    W = wA + wB
    wsum = float(np.sum(W))
    quad = 2.0 * float(p0) * h

    if wsum > 1e-15 and n > 1:
        a = W[1:]
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * f, N).real
        left = conv[:n]

        ar = a[::-1]
        fa_rev = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
        convr = np.fft.irfft(fa_rev * f, N).real
        right = convr[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

        quad = quad + left + right

    G = term - quad
    grad = dx * G
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(L), grad


def sum_constraint(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - (int(np.asarray(x).size) / 2.0))


def sum_jacobian(x):
    return np.ones(np.asarray(x).size, dtype=float)


def slsqp_fun(x):
    L, _ = lse_and_grad(x, _OPT['beta'])
    return float(L)


def slsqp_grad(x):
    _, g = lse_and_grad(x, _OPT['beta'])
    return g


def slsqp_anneal(n, h0, max_time):
    start = time.time()
    h = make_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h)

    if n <= 180:
        scales = (20, 100, 500)
        its = (50, 50, 40)
    elif n <= 280:
        scales = (20, 100)
        its = (35, 30)
    else:
        scales = (100,)
        its = (20,)

    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sum_constraint, 'jac': sum_jacobian}]
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)

    for i in range(len(scales)):
        elapsed = time.time() - start
        if elapsed > max_time:
            break
        _OPT['beta'] = float(scales[i]) / float(n)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                slsqp_fun,
                h,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                jac=slsqp_grad,
                options={'maxiter': int(its[i]), 'ftol': 1e-12, 'iprint': 0}
            )
            h2 = make_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            if c2 < best_c + 0.002:
                h = h2
            else:
                h = best_h.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
        if time.time() - start > max_time:
            break

    return best_h, best_c


def fista_lse(n, h0, beta, max_time):
    start = time.time()
    h = make_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    current_h = h.copy()
    current_c = best_c

    if max_time <= 0.0 or n <= 0:
        return best_h, current_h, best_c

    base_step = 5.0
    step = base_step
    stuck = 0
    max_iter = 100000

    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() - start > max_time:
            break

        L, grad = lse_and_grad(current_h, beta)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break
        if float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) <= 1e-16:
            break

        accepted = False
        trial_step = step
        for _ in range(12):
            y = make_feasible(current_h - trial_step * grad, n)
            Ly = lse_eval(y, beta)
            if np.isfinite(Ly) and Ly <= L + 1e-13:
                current_h = y
                accepted = True
                break
            trial_step *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            base_step = max(1e-4, base_step * 0.5)
            step = base_step
            stuck += 1
            if stuck > 30 or base_step < 1e-7:
                break
            continue

        stuck = 0
        step = min(5.0, step * 1.05)

        if (it % 20) == 0 or it == 0:
            current_c = compute_c5(current_h)
            if current_c < best_c:
                best_c = current_c
                best_h = current_h.copy()

    current_c = compute_c5(current_h)
    if current_c < best_c:
        best_c = current_c
        best_h = current_h.copy()
    return best_h, current_h, best_c


def fista_anneal(n, h0, max_time):
    start = time.time()
    x = make_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = x.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(x)

    if max_time >= 240.0:
        scales = (2, 10, 50, 200, 1000, 3000)
        weights = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 4)
    elif max_time >= 90.0:
        scales = (5, 50, 200, 1000)
        weights = (1, 2, 3, 4)
    elif max_time >= 35.0:
        scales = (10, 100, 1000)
        weights = (1, 2, 4)
    else:
        scales = (100, 1000)
        weights = (1, 5)

    total_w = float(sum(weights))
    for i in range(len(scales)):
        elapsed = time.time() - start
        if max_time - elapsed < 5.0:
            break
        w = float(weights[i])
        slice_t = max_time * (w / total_w)
        slice_t = min(slice_t, max_time - elapsed)
        if slice_t < 3.0:
            continue
        beta = float(scales[i]) / float(n)
        phase_best, phase_final, phase_c = fista_lse(n, x, beta, slice_t)
        if phase_c < best_c:
            best_c = phase_c
            best_h = phase_best.copy()

        c_final = compute_c5(phase_final)
        if c_final < best_c + 0.015:
            x = phase_final
        else:
            x = best_h.copy()

    return best_h, x, best_c


def local_anneal(n, h0, max_time, seed=42):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = make_feasible(h0, n)
    c_cur = compute_c5(x)
    best_h = x.copy()
    best_c = c_cur
    target = n / 2.0

    if n < 2 or max_time <= 0.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    start = time.time()
    step_start = 0.05
    t_start = 0.0025
    step_min = 0.0005
    t_min = 1e-4
    step = step_start
    T = t_start
    no_accept = 0
    max_iter = 1000000

    for it in range(max_iter):
        if (it % 100) == 0 and (time.time() - start) > max_time:
            break
        if (it % 400) == 0:
            decay = math.pow(0.985, float(it // 400))
            step = max(step_min, step_start * decay)
            T = max(t_min, t_start * decay)

        if no_accept > 120:
            step = max(step_min, step * 0.85)
            T = max(t_min, T * 0.85)
            no_accept = 0

        mode = rng.random()
        if mode < 0.22:
            m = int(rng.randint(1, 6))
            m = min(m, n)
            y = x.copy()
            idxs = rng.choice(n, m, replace=False)
            for idx in idxs:
                y[int(idx)] = y[int(idx)] + rng.uniform(-step, step)
            y = make_feasible(y, n)
        else:
            i = int(rng.randint(n))
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            amt = rng.uniform(0.0, max(step, 1e-6))
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                src, dst = i, j
            else:
                src, dst = j, i
            max_move = min(amt, float(x[src]), 1.0 - float(x[dst]))
            if max_move <= 1e-8:
                continue
            y = x.copy()
            y[src] = max(0.0, y[src] - max_move)
            y[dst] = min(1.0, y[dst] + max_move)

        c_new = compute_c5(y)
        if c_new <= c_cur:
            x = y
            c_cur = c_new
            no_accept = 0
            if c_new < best_c:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = x.copy()
        else:
            diff = c_new - c_cur
            accept_prob = math.exp(-max(0.0, diff) / max(T, 1e-9))
            if rng.random() < accept_prob:
                x = y
                c_cur = c_new
                if c_new < best_c:
                    best_c = c_new
                    best_h = x.copy()
            else:
                no_accept += 1

        if (it % 2000) == 0 and c_cur > best_c + 0.003:
            x = best_h.copy()
            c_cur = best_c

    return best_h, best_c


def resample(h_src, n_dst):
    h_src = np.asarray(h_src, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n_src = int(h_src.size)
    n_dst = int(n_dst)
    if n_dst <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    if n_src == n_dst:
        return h_src.copy()
    if n_src == 1:
        return np.full(n_dst, float(h_src[0]), dtype=float)
    if n_dst % n_src == 0:
        m = n_dst // n_src
        return np.repeat(h_src, m)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_src, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_dst, endpoint=False)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_src)
    return y


def generate_candidates(n, rng, max_count=12):
    cands = []
    if n < 16:
        return cands

    half = n // 2

    # Random continuous.
    h = rng.rand(n)
    cands.append(make_feasible(h, n))

    # Random binary.
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if half > 0:
        ones = rng.choice(n, half, replace=False)
        h[ones] = 1.0
    cands.append(make_feasible(h, n))

    # Wiedemann-type triangular construction.
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        t = (i * (i + 1) // 2) % n
        if t < half:
            h[i] = 1.0
    cands.append(make_feasible(h, n))

    # Quadratic modular constructions.
    for a in (3, 5, 11, 13):
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for i in range(n):
            s = (i * i + a * i) % n
            if s < half:
                h[i] = 1.0
        cands.append(make_feasible(h, n))

    # Smooth low-frequency sinusoidal candidates.
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    for w in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
        for ph in (0.0, 0.5):
            f = np.sin(np.pi * w * x + np.pi * ph)
            v = f - float(np.mean(f))
            m = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
            if m > 1e-10:
                v = 0.88 * v / m
            h = 0.5 + 0.5 * v
            cands.append(make_feasible(h, n))

    if max_count > 0 and len(cands) > int(max_count):
        cands = cands[:int(max_count)]
    return cands


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    # Get parent when available.
    h_best = None
    try:
        hp = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
        if hp.size > 0:
            h_best = hp.copy()
    except Exception:
        h_best = None

    if h_best is None or h_best.size == 0:
        n_fallback = 160
        h_best = np.zeros(n_fallback, dtype=float)
        for i in range(n_fallback):
            t = (i * (i + 1) // 2) % n_fallback
            if t < n_fallback // 2:
                h_best[i] = 1.0

    n_best = int(h_best.size)
    n_best = make_feasible(h_best, n_best).size
    h_best = make_feasible(h_best, n_best)
    c_best = compute_c5(h_best)

    # Optional alternative starting basins at current resolution.
    alt1_h = None
    alt1_c = 1e100
    alt2_h = None
    alt2_c = 1e100

    rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 360 and n_best <= 320:
        cands = generate_candidates(n_best, rng, 12)
        for hc in cands:
            if rem <= 320:
                break
            hc = make_feasible(hc, n_best)
            if float(np.mean(np.abs(hc - h_best))) < 1e-5:
                continue
            cc = compute_c5(hc)
            if cc < alt1_c:
                alt2_h, alt2_c = alt1_h, alt1_c
                alt1_h, alt1_c = hc, cc
            elif cc < alt2_c:
                alt2_h, alt2_c = hc, cc
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)

    # Main resolution optimization from the parent / current best.
    rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 160:
        fista_time = min(240.0, max(35.0, rem - 220.0))
        h_f, _, c_f = fista_anneal(n_best, h_best, fista_time)
        if c_f < c_best:
            h_best = h_f
            n_best = int(h_best.size)
            c_best = c_f
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)

    rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 120 and n_best <= 300:
        slsqp_time = min(130.0, max(25.0, rem - 95.0))
        h_s, c_s = slsqp_anneal(n_best, h_best, slsqp_time)
        if c_s < c_best:
            h_best = h_s
            n_best = int(h_best.size)
            c_best = c_s
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)

    rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 220:
        local_time = min(200.0, max(35.0, rem - 120.0))
        h_l, c_l = local_anneal(n_best, h_best, local_time, seed=int(seed) + 1)
        if c_l < c_best:
            h_best = h_l
            n_best = int(h_best.size)
            c_best = c_l
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)

    # Try the best alternative basin if there is enough time.
    rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 130 and alt1_h is not None and len(alt1_h) == n_best:
        alt_time = min(130.0, max(25.0, rem - 75.0))
        h_a, _, c_a = fista_anneal(n_best, alt1_h, alt_time)
        if c_a < c_best:
            h_best = h_a
            n_best = int(h_best.size)
            c_best = c_a
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
        if rem > 80 and n_best <= 260:
            h_as, c_as = slsqp_anneal(n_best, h_best, min(70.0, max(15.0, rem - 45.0)))
            if c_as < c_best:
                h_best = h_as
                n_best = int(h_best.size)
                c_best = c_as
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)

    # Optional second alternative.
    rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 170 and alt2_h is not None and len(alt2_h) == n_best:
        alt_time = min(90.0, max(20.0, rem - 80.0))
        h_a, _, c_a = fista_anneal(n_best, alt2_h, alt_time)
        if c_a < c_best:
            h_best = h_a
            n_best = int(h_best.size)
            c_best = c_a
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)

    # Resolution sweep.
    n_opts = []
    base = int(n_best)
    for v in (int(base * 1.5), base * 2, 240, 320, 512, 640, 1024):
        if v < 64 or v > 1200:
            continue
        if v % 2 != 0:
            v += 1
        if v == base:
            continue
        exists = False
        for u in n_opts:
            if u == v:
                exists = True
                break
        if not exists:
            n_opts.append(v)
    n_opts.sort()

    for n_try in n_opts:
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
        if rem < 120:
            break

        h_init = resample(h_best, n_try)
        h_init = make_feasible(h_init, n_try)

        fista_time = min(140.0, max(25.0, rem - 90.0))
        h_f, _, c_f = fista_anneal(n_try, h_init, fista_time)
        if c_f < c_best:
            h_best = h_f
            n_best = n_try
            c_best = c_f
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)

        if rem > 120 and n_try <= 280:
            slsqp_time = min(80.0, max(15.0, rem - 65.0))
            h_s, c_s = slsqp_anneal(n_try, h_best, slsqp_time)
            if c_s < c_best:
                h_best = h_s
                n_best = n_try
                c_best = c_s
        rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)

        if rem > 90 and n_try <= 420:
            local_time = min(70.0, max(15.0, rem - 45.0))
            h_l, c_l = local_anneal(n_try, h_best, local_time, seed=int(seed) + n_try)
            if c_l < c_best:
                h_best = h_l
                n_best = n_try
                c_best = c_l

    # Final high-temperature polished but nearly sharp smooth pass.
    rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 75:
        beta_final = 3000.0 / max(1, int(n_best))
        h_f, _, c_f = fista_lse(n_best, h_best, beta_final, min(55.0, max(10.0, rem - 25.0)))
        if c_f < c_best:
            h_best = h_f
            n_best = int(h_best.size)
            c_best = c_f

    # Final exact hill climb.
    rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 55:
        h_l, c_l = local_anneal(n_best, h_best, min(35.0, max(8.0, rem - 10.0)), seed=int(seed) + 777)
        if c_l < c_best:
            h_best = h_l
            n_best = int(h_best.size)
            c_best = c_l

    # Final feasibility and evaluation.
    h_best = make_feasible(h_best, n_best)
    c_best = compute_c5(h_best)

    # Very small rescue if final feasibility shifted the value.
    rem = budget - (time.time() - start_time)
    if rem > 15 and c_best > 0.38097:
        h_l, c_l = local_anneal(n_best, h_best, min(10.0, max(3.0, rem - 5.0)), seed=int(seed) + 31337)
        if c_l < c_best:
            h_best = make_feasible(h_l, n_best)
            c_best = compute_c5(h_best)

    return (h_best, float(c_best), int(n_best))
```
